LITTLE TONAWANDA CREEK AT LINDEN NY, USGS site 04216500 (USGS-04216500), stream in Genesee County, New York, draining 22.1 square miles. Daily means from 1912-08-01 to 1992-09-30, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
